% greyscaleAGNbands.tex
% unified model of active galactic nuclei, line of sight unification, with emission rings, greyscale
% standalone, compile with pdflatex
%
% layout of this file
%   1  geometry
%   2  palette, styles and shadings
%   3  helper macros
%   4  cloud positions
%   5  the figure
%   6  torus surface data, shaded patches sorted from far to near
%   7  document

\documentclass[tikz,border=6pt]{standalone}
\usepackage[scaled]{helvet}
\renewcommand{\familydefault}{\sfdefault}
\usetikzlibrary{arrows.meta,fadings,decorations.text}


% 1  geometry

% torus with major radius R and tube semi axis A in the disk plane, seen from elevation e,
% the sketch is rotated by tilt on the page
\def\R{2.9}
\def\A{1.1}
\def\e{17}
\def\tilt{25}
% screen angle of the torus edge above the disk plane, where lines of sight start to graze the torus
\def\lam{24.0}
% jet tip, centre line wiggle xc and half width wj along the jet coordinate y
\def\ytop{9.0}
\tikzset{declare function={
  xc(\y,\ph)=0.045*\y*sin(88*\y+\ph);
  wj(\y)=1.25*(0.03+0.016*\y+0.0019*\y^3)*sqrt(max(0,1-(\y/\ytop)^3))*(1+0.1*sin(215*\y)*(\y/\ytop)^2);
}}
% annotation radii, all centred on the black hole
\pgfmathsetmacro{\ra}{9.6}            % observer arc, inner edge of the emission rings
\pgfmathsetmacro{\rdash}{\R+\A+0.3}   % dashed disk plane line starts just outside the torus
% emission rings with band width bw, gap bg between bands, extra gap lg between the line and
% continuum bands, angular gap sg between sectors, and nb bands
\pgfmathsetmacro{\bw}{0.40}
\pgfmathsetmacro{\bg}{0.05}
\pgfmathsetmacro{\lg}{0.14}
\pgfmathsetmacro{\sg}{1.4}
\def\nb{7}
\pgfmathsetmacro{\rout}{\ra+\nb*(\bw+\bg)-\bg+\lg}
\let\robs\rout                        % viewer arrows start outside the rings


% 2  palette, styles and shadings

% greyscale
\tikzset{
  disk/.style={inner color=white,outer color=black!50,draw=black!60,line width=0.3pt},
  cut/.style={fill=black!14,draw=black!55,line width=0.4pt},
  blr/.style={top color=black!55,bottom color=black!72},
  nlr/.style={top color=black!16,bottom color=black!30},
}
\colorlet{corona}{white}
% jet shadings, the shading box maps 25bp to 75bp onto the jet, so it diffuses into white from about halfway
\pgfdeclareverticalshading{jetup}{100bp}{color(0bp)=(black!85);color(25bp)=(black!85);color(34bp)=(black!60);
  color(44bp)=(black!32);color(53bp)=(black!14);color(60bp)=(black!5);color(66bp)=(white);color(100bp)=(white)}
\pgfdeclareverticalshading{jetdown}{100bp}{color(0bp)=(white);color(34bp)=(white);color(40bp)=(black!5);
  color(47bp)=(black!14);color(56bp)=(black!32);color(66bp)=(black!60);color(75bp)=(black!85);color(100bp)=(black!85)}
% emission bands, inner to outer, the two line bands first, then the continuum by energy,
% all bands share one grey ramp and are identified by position and name
\colorlet{b0}{black}\colorlet{b1}{black}\colorlet{b2}{black}\colorlet{b3}{black}
\colorlet{b4}{black}\colorlet{b5}{black}\colorlet{b6}{black}
\colorlet{bbase}{white}
\def\levpct{{7,24,42,60}}             % none, low, mid, high
% band name text, dark on the mid gamma ray band of the labelled sector
\colorlet{t0}{white}\colorlet{t1}{white}\colorlet{t2}{black!85}\colorlet{t3}{white}
\colorlet{t4}{white}\colorlet{t5}{white}\colorlet{t6}{white}

\tikzset{
  lab/.style={font=\small,inner sep=1pt},
  lead/.style={line width=0.4pt,black,preaction={draw=white,line width=2pt,line cap=round}},
  obs/.style={-{Stealth[length=6pt]},line width=0.6pt},
}
% corona fades from nearly opaque at its centre to fully transparent at its edge
\tikzfading[name=coronafade,inner color=transparent!8,outer color=transparent!100]


% 3  helper macros

% torus surface patch with corners #2 to #5 and #1 percent black
\newcommand{\tp}[5]{\filldraw[black!#1,line width=0.2pt,line join=round] (#2) -- (#3) -- (#4) -- (#5) -- cycle;}

% rounded, slightly irregular cloud of radius #1 with style #2
\newcommand{\cloud}[2]{\shade[#2] plot[smooth cycle,tension=0.9,domain=0:315,samples=8,variable=\t]
  ({#1*(1+0.08*rand)*cos(\t)},{#1*(1+0.08*rand)*sin(\t)});}

% clouds at the positions in list #1 with radius #2, style #3 and random seed #4 for their outlines
\newcommand{\putclouds}[4]{\pgfmathsetseed{#4}
  \foreach \x/\y in #1{\begin{scope}[shift={(\x,\y)}]\cloud{#2}{#3}\end{scope}}}

% jet on side #1 (1 up, -1 down) with wiggle phase #2 and shading #3,
% widening continuously and diffusing into white towards its rounded end
\newcommand{\jet}[3]{\shade[shading=#3,yscale=#1]
  plot[domain=0.1:\ytop,samples=320,smooth,variable=\y] ({xc(\y,#2)-wj(\y)},{\y})
  -- plot[domain=\ytop:0.1,samples=320,smooth,variable=\y] ({xc(\y,#2)+wj(\y)},{\y}) -- cycle;}

% viewer arrow at the centre of the range from #1 to #2 degrees from the upper jet axis with label #3,
% the arrow is radial and the label is anchored on its side facing the centre
\newcommand{\obs}[3]{
  \pgfmathsetmacro{\ph}{90+\tilt-(#1+#2)/2}
  \pgfmathsetmacro{\anc}{\ph+180}
  \draw[obs] (\ph:\robs+1.2) -- (\ph:\robs+0.25);
  \node[lab,anchor=\anc,align=left,inner sep=4pt] at (\ph:\robs+1.2) {#3};}
\newcommand{\sub}[1]{{\scriptsize\color{black!65}#1}}

% emission ring sector from #1 to #2 degrees from the upper jet axis, #3 and #4 set whether each end
% gets a gap, #5 lists the strength levels 0 to 3 from the inner to the outer band
\newcommand{\sector}[5]{
  \pgfmathsetmacro{\aone}{90+\tilt-(#1)-#3*\sg/2}\pgfmathsetmacro{\atwo}{90+\tilt-(#2)+#4*\sg/2}
  \foreach \lv [count=\n from 0] in {#5}{
    \pgfmathsetmacro{\rone}{\ra+\n*(\bw+\bg)+(\n>1)*\lg}\pgfmathsetmacro{\rtwo}{\rone+\bw}
    \pgfmathtruncatemacro{\pc}{\levpct[\lv]}
    \fill[b\n!\pc!bbase] (\aone:\rone) arc[start angle=\aone,end angle=\atwo,radius=\rone]
      -- (\atwo:\rtwo) arc[start angle=\atwo,end angle=\aone,radius=\rtwo] -- cycle;
  }}


% 4  cloud positions

% broad line region above and below the disk, placed so that clouds behind the disk or torus
% stay clear of them, and narrow line region further out, in the frame of the rotated sketch
\def\blrup{
  0.322/1.002,-0.445/0.910,0.601/1.478,0.338/1.548,
  -0.362/1.566,-0.606/1.478,-0.781/1.262,0.954/1.866,
  0.447/2.068,-0.500/2.083,-0.744/1.995,-0.887/1.771,
  1.106/2.513,0.818/2.429,0.632/2.779,-0.635/2.750,
  -0.946/2.633,-1.136/2.488,-1.456/2.430,1.272/2.940,
  0.755/3.272,-1.049/3.117,-1.381/3.190,-1.487/2.802}
\def\blrlo{
  0.343/-1.028,-0.437/-0.939,0.580/-1.452,0.354/-1.586,
  -0.571/-1.351,-0.502/-1.755,0.687/-1.747,0.773/-2.054,
  0.452/-2.019,-0.757/-2.074,-1.009/-2.211,1.094/-2.294,
  0.929/-2.620,0.618/-2.675,-0.645/-2.618,-0.880/-2.486,
  -1.132/-2.511,-1.292/-2.236,1.664/-3.054,1.296/-2.936,
  0.981/-3.070,0.748/-3.195,-0.787/-3.150,-1.044/-3.252,
  -1.241/-3.012}
\def\nlrup{
  2.928/3.283,2.085/3.772,1.327/3.875,0.773/4.328,
  -0.817/4.323,-1.490/3.899,-2.170/3.550,-2.300/4.550,
  2.526/4.451,1.681/4.628,1.054/5.247,-1.762/4.957,
  3.964/4.404,3.526/4.835,2.709/5.241,2.126/5.637,
  -1.366/5.670,-2.363/5.722,-2.852/4.228}
\def\nlrlo{
  -2.124/-3.762,-1.503/-4.170,2.326/-3.751,2.865/-3.343,
  -3.306/-4.180,-2.543/-4.700,-1.721/-4.744,-1.183/-4.991,
  1.150/-4.882,1.726/-4.809,2.552/-4.358,3.013/-4.108,
  3.730/-3.726,-3.342/-4.913,-2.033/-5.492,-1.526/-5.749,
  1.526/-5.760,2.227/-5.491,2.655/-5.124,3.382/-4.911}


% 5  the figure

\newcommand{\agnfigure}{%
\begin{tikzpicture}[font=\small]

\begin{scope}[rotate=\tilt]

  \torusback

  % far side first, the lower jet and lower clouds sit behind the disk
  \jet{-1}{180}{jetdown}
  \putclouds{\blrlo}{0.085}{blr}{4}
  \putclouds{\nlrlo}{0.2}{nlr}{5}

  % accretion disk as a thin sheet reaching the inner rim of the torus
  \pgfmathsetmacro{\rd}{\R-\A}\pgfmathsetmacro{\bd}{\rd*sin(\e)}
  \shade[disk] (0,0) ellipse[x radius=\rd,y radius=\bd];

  % near side, upper clouds and upper jet
  \putclouds{\blrup}{0.085}{blr}{9}
  \putclouds{\nlrup}{0.2}{nlr}{10}
  \jet{1}{0}{jetup}

  % hot corona as a light, diffuse dome above the disk
  \begin{scope}
    \clip (-0.85,0) arc[start angle=180,end angle=360,x radius=0.85,y radius={0.85*sin(\e)}]
      -- (0.85,1.2) -- (-0.85,1.2) -- cycle;
    \fill[corona,path fading=coronafade] (0,0) circle (0.85);
  \end{scope}

  % black hole as a sphere of radius rs sunk by cs into the disk, the sheet hides the part below it
  \pgfmathsetmacro{\rs}{0.19}\pgfmathsetmacro{\cs}{0.07}
  \pgfmathsetmacro{\ri}{sqrt(\rs*\rs-\cs*\cs)}\pgfmathsetmacro{\as}{atan2(-\cs,\ri)}
  \fill[black] (-\ri,0) arc[start angle=180,end angle=360,x radius=\ri,y radius={\ri*sin(\e)}]
    arc[start angle=\as,end angle=180-\as,radius=\rs] -- cycle;

  \torusfront

  % pointer anchors
  \coordinate (aJet)  at ({xc(6.0,0)-0.4*wj(6.0)},6.0);
  \coordinate (aNLR)  at (124:5.1);
  \coordinate (aBLR)  at (-0.887,1.771);
  \coordinate (aCor)  at (-0.15,0.45);
  \coordinate (aBH)   at (-0.1,0.12);
  \coordinate (aDisk) at (-0.6,-0.3);
  \coordinate (aTor)  at (-3.4,0.35);
\end{scope}

% disk plane separating the radio loud and radio quiet halves, labels centred on the dashed segment
\draw[line width=0.5pt,dash pattern=on 4pt off 3pt] (\tilt:\rdash) -- (\tilt:\ra);
\pgfmathsetmacro{\rmid}{(\rdash+\ra)/2}
\node[lab,font=\scriptsize,rotate=\tilt,anchor=south,inner sep=3.5pt] at (\tilt:\rmid) {Radio Loud};
\node[lab,font=\scriptsize,rotate=\tilt,anchor=north,inner sep=3.5pt] at (\tilt:\rmid) {Radio Quiet};

% component labels in one aligned column on the left with equal spacing
\def\xl{-7.6}
\foreach \a/\i/\txt in {aJet/0/Jet,aNLR/1/Narrow Line Region,aBLR/2/Broad Line Region,
  aCor/3/Hot Corona,aBH/4/Black Hole,aDisk/5/Accretion Disk,aTor/6/Dusty Torus}{
  \pgfmathsetmacro{\yl}{5.0-1.2*\i}
  \draw[lead] (\a) -- (\xl+0.4,\yl) -- (\xl,\yl);
  \node[lab,anchor=east] at (\xl-0.05,\yl) {\txt};
}

% emission rings, one sector per viewing range, levels from the inner to the outer band
%                              broad narrow gamma x-ray opt/uv ir radio
\sector{0}{15}{1}{1}           {1,1,3,3,2,2,3}
\sector{15}{90-\lam}{1}{1}     {3,3,2,3,3,3,3}
\sector{90-\lam}{90}{1}{1}     {0,3,1,1,1,2,3}
\sector{90}{90+\lam}{1}{1}     {0,3,0,1,1,2,1}
% seyfert 1 range split without gaps into intermediate types, from the torus edge towards the axis
\sector{90+\lam}{124}{1}{0}    {1,3,0,2,1,3,1}  % 1.9
\sector{124}{136}{0}{0}        {2,3,0,2,1,3,1}  % 1.8
\sector{136}{154}{0}{0}        {2,3,0,3,2,3,1}  % 1.5
\sector{154}{180}{0}{1}        {3,3,0,3,3,3,1}  % 1

% band names along the radio loud quasar sector, centred radially in each band
\pgfmathsetmacro{\aone}{90+\tilt-15-\sg}\pgfmathsetmacro{\atwo}{90+\tilt-(90-\lam)+\sg}
\foreach \txt [count=\n from 0] in {Broad Lines,Narrow Lines,Gamma Ray,X-Ray,Optical / UV,Infrared,Radio}{
  \pgfmathsetmacro{\rm}{\ra+\n*(\bw+\bg)+(\n>1)*\lg+\bw/2}
  \path[decorate,decoration={text along path,text align=center,raise=-2.5pt,
    text={|\scriptsize\color{t\n}|\txt}}] (\aone:\rm) arc[start angle=\aone,end angle=\atwo,radius=\rm];
}

% viewer classes
\obs{0}{15}{Blazar\\[-1pt]\sub{BL Lac / FSRQ}}
\obs{15}{90-\lam}{Radio Loud Quasar\\[-1pt]\sub{Broad Line Radio Galaxy}}
\obs{90-\lam}{90}{Radio Galaxy\\[-1pt]\sub{Narrow Line Radio Galaxy}\\[-3pt]\sub{FR I / FR II}}
\obs{90}{90+\lam}{Seyfert 2\\[-1pt]\sub{Obscured Quasar}\\[-3pt]\sub{Type II QSO}}
\obs{90+\lam}{180}{Seyfert 1\\[-1pt]\sub{Radio Quiet Quasar}\\[-3pt]\sub{Type I QSO}}

% legend, a single row of the shared strength ramp
\begin{scope}[shift={(\xl-1.6,-5.6)}]
  \node[lab,anchor=south west] at (0,0.1) {Emission Strength};
  \foreach \h [count=\c from 0] in {None,Low,Mid,High}{
    \pgfmathtruncatemacro{\pc}{\levpct[\c]}
    \fill[b0!\pc!bbase] (\c*0.85,-0.45) rectangle ++(0.7,0.4);
    \node[lab,font=\scriptsize,anchor=north] at (\c*0.85+0.35,-0.5) {\h};
  }
\end{scope}

\end{tikzpicture}%
}


% 6  torus surface data

% the torus surface is a parametric torus with an elliptical tube, R 2.9, radial semi axis 1.1,
% vertical semi axis 1.25, cut open by 24 degrees on either side of the line of sight, split into
% small quads that are Lambert shaded with a soft highlight, back face culled and sorted from far
% to near, so that drawing them in order hides what lies behind

% far half of the torus, drawn before the disk, jets and clouds
